## I 、 Letter to Shareholders

## 1. Implementation Results of 2022 Business Plan

The turover of our company this year is NT$ 5,015,224 thousand dollars, it has increased for  16.07%  comparing  with  that  in  2021  (NT$  4,320,822  thousand  dollars).  Net  operating profit is NT$ 1,830,038 dollars, it has increased 27.45%, comparing with that in 2021 (NT$ 1,435,925  thousand  dollars.  Profit  before  tax  is  NT$  2,048,608  thousand  dollars,  it  has increased  44.01%  comparing  with  that  in  2021  (NT$  1,422,586  thousand  dollars).  Profit before tax belonging to parent company is NT$ 1,537,649 thousand dollars, it has increased for 43.95% comparing with that in 2021 (NT$ 1,068,171 thousand dollars)

The turover of our company this year has increased for 16.07% comparing with that in 2021.  Detection  revenue  which  belongs  to  high  gorss  profit  has  increased  from  NT$ 3,865,320,000  dollars  (2021)  to  NT$  4,581,880,000  dollars  (2022),  which  has  increased 18.54%. Part selling has decreased from NT$ 455,500,000 (2021) to NT$ 433,350,000 (2022), which has decreased for 4.86%. The gross profit ratio  of  our  company  in  2022  is  54.99%, which  has  increased  for  4.96%  comparing  with  that  in  2021  (50.03%).  Profit  margin  is 36.49%, which has increased for 3.26% comparing with that in 2021 (3.26%). EPS after tax is NT$ 15.85 dollars 。 The company's competitiveness of the 5GHz terminal divice of the mobile communication net and wireless Netcom products in mobile communications is the best of the field. There were 92 for the high-end 5GHz, and the market share was as high as 32%. There were 36 for Wi-Fi 6E embarked by flagship wireless router, and the market share was as high as 48%. We have been approved by Top 20 clients in network communication industry. The company's technical capabilities, sufficient capacity and the laboratory qualification designated by international big factories in wireless netcom and mobile communications will become one of the important sources which contribute to the company's revenue growth in the 5G Mobile Communications and AI Intelligence of Things which are used in non-consumer electronics  field,  such  as  health  care  network,  industial  network,  Internet  of  Vehicles, commercial use, smart home, and 5G verticals.

The  world-wide  internetwork  industry  has  grown  vigirously,  an  industry  upgrade  and digital  transition  program  must  be  executed.  Nowadays,  it  is  true  that  5GHz  and  AI Intelligence of Things has expanded in smart Internet of Things industry and led a variety of industry  into  a  new  era  of  smart  Internet  of  Things.  According  to  the  latest  statistics  from Global mobile Suppliers Association, by the end of 2022, there is an accumulative total of 1709 devices that are end products of 5G in the world. The share of mobile phone category is 51.7%.  The  dispute  of  interdisciplinary  application  such  as  FWA,  CPE,  tablets,  laptops, drones, head-mounted display, robots, and  camera which are other 5G products not mobile phone  category  has  continuously  increased.  The  wireless  testing  technique  developed  in technology  industry  has  passed  through  the  generations  of  2G,3G,4G,  and  5G  mobile communication  technique  and  Wi-Fi  4,5,6,6E,  and  7of  area  broadband  technique.  The company has made lots of upgrades, transitions, and plans. Therefore, our company has built top laboratories which are able to provide complete tests and solutions to the top brands for

5G high-end internetwork terminal products around the world. China is the biggest market for electric vehicles.In conjunction with the completion of our own factory in Kunshan, China in 2018,  the  company  expanded  its  range  of  testing  automotive  electronics  and  combined  the advantage  of  testing  5GHz  terminal  divice  of  the  mobile  communication  net  to  meet  the requirement of testing automotive wireless products for smart cars causing by V2X internet of vehicles in the future. We have planned for the future market opportunity of testing smart cars. Our company set up laboratories in the west coast of South America in 2018. It was the first time the company set up laboratories outside Asia. The Company obtained A2LA certification and  the  municipal government business license at the end of November 2018, and officially opened in January 2019. Besides enforcing our service for our top client in the United States, our company planed in advance to obtain partnerships of the newest 5GNR and Wi-Fi 6E/7 technique of testing wireless communication. This capability was presented in the first half of 2019 where the company took the lead in obtaining the world's first US FCC certificate for 5G NR mm Wave (millimeter wave) mobile phones, and the first EU CE-RED certificate for 5G NR  Sub-6GHz  cell  phone.  In  addition,  the  Company  also  took  the  lead  in  obtaining  the world's  first  US  FCC  certificate  for  5G  NR  mm  Wave  (millimeter  wave)  CPE  products (Customer Premise Equipment) in August of the same year, and obtained the first US FCC certificate for 5G NR mmWave (millimeter wave) Notebook in 2020. Since the 5G test is very difficult  and  the  test  items  are  complex,  the  inspection  cost  will  be  greatly  increased.  The primary  brands  from  Europe,  America,  Japan,  and  China  consider  our  laboratory  as  their priority when they research and develop 5G terminal network products due to our lead in the world. Our company rapidly collects a variety of 5G network products from the manufacturers and original equipment manufacturer around the world to our laboratory and tests the products. It  has  created  the  growth  of  revenue  and  gross  profit  ratio  of  the  company.  Due  to  the slowdown in sales growth in the Sub 6G terminal market Sub 6G, wafer plants and terminal system  plants  have  developed  the  market  of  5G  mmWave  in  the  second  half  of  2022  and expect to promote a new wave of market demand for specification upgrades. In the end of 2022, Sport on started to receive orders for terminal testing the latest 5G mmWave wafers, and it was accrued to be Q1 profit in 2023. The cost of testing one 5G mmWave is way higher than the cost of testing Sub 6G, so the orders for testing specification upgrades will increase profits next year for Sporton.

As  for  the  test  of  Area  broadband  products,  the  wireless  technique  of  802.11  ax  has entered a 'tri-band' era. The original frequency band of 2.4GHza nd5GHz will apply with 6 GHz. The manufacturer of network communication wafers started to upgrade the standard of high-end wafers from dual frequency used by Wi-Fi 6E to tri-band used by Wi-Fi 6E this year and shipped a great quantity of products. It will have a big increase on the market share of Wi-Fi 6E products. Since Wi-Fi 6E is a whole new frequency band, the pattern and time of test will double. The revenue and profit of our company will be increased since the company did not increase the testing equipment and quick testing combined the newest automation of test  for  Wi-Fi  6E.  Our  company  assisted  Broadcom  to  obtain  the  first  FCC  certificate  for Wi-Fi 6E in the world in early Feburary,2020. In December, 2020, our company has worked

with ASUS and obtained the first FCC certificate for indoor low power router of Wi-Fi 6E. Since  our  company  has  the  ability  of  testing  module  wafers  and  whole  products  and  has  a plentiful  of  experience  in  testing  netword  communication,  our  company  has  the  advantages and competitiveness to be in a lead. The new generation of standard of wireless communication  technology  Wi-Fi  7  is  also  called  Standard  of    IEEE  802.11be  EHT （ Extremely  High  Throughput ） which  led  into  technology  such  as  320MHz  bandwidth, 096-QAM  modulation, Multi-RU, multi-link operation, MU-MIMO  enforcement,  and multi-AP coordination based on Wi-Fi 6E. Wi-Fi 7 will have faster rate of data signalling and lower delay compared to Wi-Fi 6E. These specifications will lead to more compound testing items with muti-mode, multi-antenna, and multichannel and testing hours, which will greatly increase  the  growth  of  revenue  and  profit  for  business  of  testing  network  communication. The Company has taken the lead in the latest Wi-Fi 7 testing market again. The Company has assist  Broadcom  to  gain  the  first  Wi-Fi7  FCC  certificate  in  the  world  in  December,  2022. Moreover, the Company cooperated with ASUS and gained the first FCC certificate of Wi-Fi 7  indoor  low-power  route  (AP)  in  the  world  in  February,  2023,  once  again  taking  the advantage in the field of high-end network communication testing. They will continuously be a  power  for  the  Company's  operation  with  5G  netcom  detection  keeps  developing  and evaluating.

For  the  transaction  of  antimagnetic  components,  the  revenue  in  2022  is  NT$  433,348 dollars, which decreased 4.86% compared to 2021. It is mainly due to the rapid decrease of market  demands.The  clients  came  to  the  stage  of  adjusting  inventory  and  the  main  clients purchase motivation is below expectation. In 2023, the Company will be actively promoting new application design to the market, and the Company plans to establish a complete team of FAE to head into the market of protection component needed by the EMC certificate, which can  reenforce  the  current  clients  and  strengthen  the  service  of  certificates  and  the  sales  of components.

## IV. 2023 Business Plan Summary

- (1) Management Guideline
1.  The  Company  actively  participates  the  meeting  for  establishing  standards  of  mobile communication  and  wireless  technology  held  by  Standard  Wireless  Association,  and make  a  layout  of  high-end  testing  equipment  to  make  sure  the  Company  has  the advantage of being in the lead in the industry. Due the the effects of COVID19, 3GPP Release 17 has been postponed until June 9, 2022 and confirm its official version. The standard of 3GPP Release 17 was declared to be officially congealed in the 96 th TSG meeting, which was    the mature and completed stage of 5G standard. According to the 3  applications  defined  by  3GPP  5G,  including  eMBB(enhanced  Mobile  Broadband) emphized the powerful wideband, uRLLC(Ultra Reliable Low Latency Communications) target to the low latency application,  and  mMTC(massive Machine Type  Communications)  connected  by  the  massive  Internet  of  Things,  bring  the prospects that we never had for 5G application. In the mean time, this version is also added the technology of non-terrestrial communication which is not the technology of communication on the ground. It is mainly used to solve the communication problem in remote mountain area or transportation on the sea. It brings the communication system such as satellites into the technology which can lead to a stereoscopic 5G internetwork. Besides, T-Mobile is going to cooperate with SpaceX, providing satellite service for the blind spots of telecommunication network. The big smart phone companies in the US have put the SOS emergency service which can deliver the data by the satellite in their products. MediaTek Inc. has promoted the first smartphone using 5G NTN satellite in the world, which can be considered to be the arrival of the new generation of application using  5G  satellite  communication  after  R17  addition.  The  5G  mobile  end  such  as smartphones, laptops, industry, medical tablets,  FWA ／ CPE drones, robots, head-mounted  displays,  will  continuously  upgrade  the  specifications  and  bring  the

related  applications.  The  next    version  i  3GPP  Release  18,  which  focases  on  the technology of 5G CA （ Carrier Aggregation ） . It will be confirmed by the end of 2023 and officially congealed in the first 2024. This addition not only conbines with the same technology of communication interface but non-continuous wave to a whole application with  lard  bandwide  and  high  speed  transmission,  but  also  integrates  Sub-6G  and millimeter  wave  which  are  two  different  mobile  communication  technologies  with different frequency band. With the integration of CA technology, it will create an upper transmission rate and high flow transmission and promote the application and upgrade of mobile communication end products. The Company has controlled the information of the  related  industries  and  keeps  making  investments.  Due  to  the  industry  competition pattern, there is a phenomenon that the stronge company stays stronge. The Company's competitive adventage is getting more obvious in the process of industry development in the 2022 revenue and profits. The trend in the future will continuously bring a bigger market of testing and profits. The Company also developes new technology for testing at  the  same  time  to  guarantee  our  world-class  competitiveness.  Currently  completed projects are as follows:

- A 、 Our company is one of the few companies which are able to provide a one-store service for testing wireless communication and mobilephones to the cilents. There for, it can decreace the problem of tesing and verifying products, and speed up the time  of  obtaining  verification,  including  the  United  States,  Canada,  European Union, Britain, and Taiwan.
- B 、 The company has obtained detection laboratory that may perform PLMN11 and IS2051 regulation testing in October, 2020. It was successfully approved by NCC in  December,  2020,  and  became  a  recognized  certification  body  (RCB),  which may be used for 5G NR wideband terminal devices and base station to provide testing services.
- C 、 Release the test and the report of mobile communication products using 5G NR FR1 SA/NSA.
- D 、 Release tests and reports of 5G NR FR2 NSA mobile communication products.
- E 、 Release tests and reports of UWB 3.1GHz~10GHz location on cellphones.
- F 、 Release mandatory tests and reports of band products using WIFI 6E 6GHz.
- G 、 Tests of throughput of Internet speed WIFI 6E 6GH
- H 、 Ability of testing OTA, such as CTIA 5G NR FR1 and FR2.
- I 、 Obtainment of testing ability and qualification of GCF/PTCRB 5G NR FR1and FR2
- J 、 Obtainment of the OTA qualification of Vodafone and Orange in Europe.
- K 、 Obtainment of the OTA qualification of AT&amp;T and Verizon in the United States.
- L 、 Obtainment of the qualification of 5G NR GCF field trial.
- M 、 Release tests and reports of 5GNR Japan Telecom Business law.

- 2 、 We develop partnerships with major customers, build new testing bases on-site with the growth of customer business, and continue to strive for the company to become the  designated  laboratory  for  more  brand  manufacturers  and  international  carriers expand the market share of the testing market.
- 3 、 The  Company  continuously  participates  the  activities  from  International  Standard Association  to  improve  the  publicity  of  Sporton  and  gain  more  right  to  establish standards of mobile communication. The Company was selected to be co-chairperson for  the  special  task  force  in  PTCRB  CTIA  OTA  MRR  in  Texas,  the  USA,  in November 9 2022., and the Company also selected to be the vice-chairperson for GCF PAG in PAG #41bis meeting held by The Global Certification Forum in Europe in 2022.  These  two  international associations are important  facilities leading the establishment of the standards of 5G Internet of Things wireless communication. The Company can grasp the chance of tesing wireless communication due to the active participation to the establishment of industry standards.
- 4 、 In order to meet the requirements of customers, we continuously develop the number of IA international certification countries and shorten the time for obtaining certificates.  Currently,  Sporton  International  INC.  has  established  IA  certification channels in more than 150 countries worldwide, and has achieved Sporton International INC. certification which is guaranteed in the world.
- 5 、 To accelerat the time of getting certificate for regions such as FCC, ISED, CE, UKCA, the lab in the USA get qualification from the FCC TCB, ISEG FCB in Canada, CE in European Union, and IKCA in Britain in 2021.
- 6 、 We develop EMC related components continuously and form a cooperation platform with related application design technologies and customers:We master the development of tablet PCs, mobile phones and the future growth trend of the Internet of Things, and cooperate with the company's testing profession to develop electromagnetic  compatible  filtering  and  protection  components  suitable  for  tablet PCs,  Internet  of  Things  and  mobile  phones  to  enhance  the  competitiveness  and increased revenue of parts sales.
- 7 、 We continue working with clients to develop the requirement of nonstandardized test, such as testing wireless throughput and cellphone field trials.
- (2) Expected sales status and its basis
- 1 、 Testing Service of Electronic Products

The  company  is  one  of  the  few  companies  in  the  world  that  may  provide complete services such as mandatory electronic products (EMC, safety regulations, and  radio  regulations),  OTA(antemma  performance),  and  compliance  (RF  and communication protocols). In response to the future electronic products which shall be  composite  products  that  include  wireless  network  communication  or  mobile communications  capabilities,  providing  a  complete  service  of  electronic  products with mandatory and conformance is one of the most important competitiveness of the company. Therefore, the following layout is made to expect that the testing services

this year may maintain a certain growth rate.

- A 、 Taiwam: The company keep investing 5G and the latest specification of network communication,  including  5G  NR  (CA)  RF/RRM  testing,  5G  NR  protocol testing, 5G NR GPS testing system (A-GPS, A-GLONASS, A-Galileo, A-BDS), 5G  NR  SIM  Card  testing  (USIM/USAT) 、 5G  NR  voice  and  voicefrequency testing  (VoNR),  5G  NR  FR,    5G  NR  FR2  (mmWave),  OTA,  CTIA  speech performance. The network communication includes 802.11ax(WiFi 6) to fit the testing requirement for the latest specification of 5G cell phones, laptops, tablets, CPE, small cell, and network communication products.
- B 、 We continue to invest 100% shareholding in the production capacity of the Subsidiary-International Certification Corp. (share). In addition to the testing capacity of WiFi 6E/7 high-end network communication products, we actively expand the production capacity and orders of LoRa network communication products, AIOT and wearable devices testing projects to expect that the revenue in 2022 may have a breakthrough growth, increasing the profitability of the company

## 2 、 Anti-magnetic electronic components sales

In  the  aspect  of  Anti-magnetic  electronic  components  sales,  in  2021,  the company  worked  with  our  brand  clients  and  has  completed  developing  TLVR Choke  applied  by  servers  in  next  generation.  In  the  latter  half  of  the  year,  the company is about to manufacture and ship a great amount of products, and reach out the related clients actively. The company has planned to set up a special production line for NFC Near-field antenna which mainly based on customerization to provide a one-stop servive from application and design to manufacturing to the major clients which  reached  our  aim:  Technology  and  service  as  priority,  competitiveness  as minority. Therefore, the company and the department can keep making progress.

3

## 、 China

To  maintain  the  long-term  competitive  advantage  and  operational  growth  kinetic energy  of  Sporton  International  INC.,  our  Kunshan  factory  not  only  added  the %GNR testing equipments for Sub 6GHz(FR1) and mmWave(FR2), integrate the testing team of developing automotive electronics to grasp the testing opportunities of  future  smart  vehicle  development,  which  makes  the  service  of  the  company's electronic  testing  expand  from  consumer  electronics  to  automotive  electronics, providing  another  source  of  growth  for  the  company's  future  operations,  while continuing to expand its capacity in Shenzhen. Sporton International INC. continues to expand  its investment  in  the  subsidiary  Holdings  Electronic  in China  to strengthen  its  technical  service  capabilities  and  test  its  production  capacity.  The growth of companies in China is one of the main sources of growth for the company in the future.

## (3) Important Production and Marketing Policy

- 1 、 Marketing
- A 、 Have  a  partnership  of  strategic  alliance  with  the  big  brand  which  is  in  the upstream  supply  chain  of  5G  and  internet  communication,  such  as  wafer manufacturer,  brand  manufacturer,  and  telecommunication  industry  in  Europe and  America.  Therefore,  the  company  can  provide  the  most  advanced  and complete test and solutions of wireless communication products.
- B 、 Participate in international organization of standard test and become a member. Meanwhile,  the  company  has  become  the  testing  laboratory  assigned  by  the organization establishing standard, and contributed the database of latest case of standard test. It has made our compony the most professional laboratory.
- C 、 Create  self-own  brand  of  'SPORTON'  lab  and  expand  to  become  a  large international  veterfication  syndicate.  The  company  expand  the  5G  and  AIOT technology  to  many  industries  and  gradually  expand  to  the  field  of  testing noncomsumable electronics,  such  as  medical  care,  industry,  chemistry,  optics, automotive  electronics,  which  are  the  industries  applying  5G  and  AIOT products.

## 2 、 Production

The company creates laboratory to provide non-stop services for  customers in China, America, and Taiwan, and provide schedule to customers to fight for the business opportunities of products going on the market.

## 3 、 Product development direction

- A 、 Test certification: With the expansion of the market for 5G application, there is a tendency that the 5G products have expanded in a variety of industry. In addition to maintain the advantage in testing technology of wireless communication  products,  with  the  requirement  of  testing  noncosumable electronics,  the  company  will  research  and  develop  testing  technology  in other  expert  field,  and  establish  regulations.  Once  again  promoting  the competitiveness of tesing and giving solutions for smart internetwork in next generation.
- B 、 Component selling: Continue to expand the proportion of customized product R&amp;D and manufacturing services, gradually establish a more complete FAE team, and at the same time develop more upstream and downstream supply chain channels, and prepare to enter the protection component market, set up independent production lines for some big customers' customized products, and  improve  products  competitiveness  and  reduce  the  loss  of  related  R&amp;D core value to make the relationship between the Company and the big clients closer.

## V. Future Company Development Strategy:

The  cross-industry  application  of  5G+AIOT  smart  networking  technology  has  been established. It is no longer the case that the consumer electronics industry applies networking products alone. Industrial network, medical network, Internet of Vehicles, smart home, vertical field,  remote  office,  metaverse  audio-visual  applications,  etc.  are  all  smart  networking application that have been actively deployed by various industries in recent years. Following the development of global technology, it is urgent to expand testing service projects and global layout in other fields. Whether it is vertical development, continuing to deepen the advantages of  testing  and  certification  in  the  original  wireless  communication  field,  or  horizontal development, cross-field testing service projects will continue to expand. The following goals are achieved:

- (A) The Most Complete Service: Provide a complete test and solutions for interindustry smart network products. In addition to the latest 5G FR1/FR2 smart phones, 5G laptops, tablets used in industry and health care network, 5G PDA for factories and logistics, 5G vertical fields (small base station such us 5G CPE, FWA, Router, Femtocell), LPWAN low delay, low  power  loss,  wearable  or  AIOT  terminal,  area  broadband  router  of  Wi-Fi  6E/7,  the company could provide  all  mandatory  and  compliance  testing  vertifying  services,  CTIA standard test for effectiveness of  antenna, PTCRB/GCF standard functionality, field trial demanded by telecommunication industry to CE, FCC and more than 200 countries in the world to satisfy the clients with one-stop shopping experience.
- (B) Localized  services:  Following  the  testing  requirement  researching  by  the  department  of researching and developing clients, the company plans to build the test laboratory aroud the world. From the Field Trial Service Office in Germany to the first laboratory across states built in western United States, they are all in order to satisfy the top brands to adapt to the customerized service quickly changed by the industry and the high competitive environment. In the mean time, the company can obtain information of new standard for products in advanced, and grasp the market opportunity.
- (C) Automated service: With the development of the wireless communication standard toward muti-antenna, muti-frequency band, extremely high speed, large broadband, requirement of testing items gradually become more complicated and the testing mode has dobouled. Our company  has  planned  to  develop  intelligence  softwares  and  given  tests  and  reports  for many years. Nowadays, the company has performed a high effectiveness of testing better than  our  competitors.  We  have  made  a  big  decrease  of  the  time  of  testing  and  making reports.  Meanwhile, we  shorten the time of operation and increase the quality of testing report to assist our clients to obtain the certificate so that they can introduce their products to the market in advanced.
- (D)  The  company  keeps  building  the  newest  equipments  for  5G  FR1/FR2,  and  we  have  the most complete testing laboratories for 5G NR in the world. We also have the latest testing technology for 5G wireless communication field in the world. In the mean time, we have the most cases of testing high-end 5G internet communication products in the world, which

is reported by Federal Communications Commission. We are also the laboratories assigned by  wafers  manufacturers  and  brand  manufacturers  of  5G  internet  communication.  The company  has  been  in  the  lead  of  testing  5G  high-end  wireless  communication.  The company keep developing other testing items for wired products to increase our advantage across  the  industry  and  gradually  become  a  large  testing  and  vertifying  organization. Impact of external competitive environment, regulatory environment and overall business environment:

Facing  the  coronavirus  epidemic  starting  this  spring,  the  explanations  for  the  impact  on company's business, countermeasures and prevention measures against the epidemic are as follows:

## 1. Test certification services:

- A 、 Sporton  in  China:  The  labs  in  KunShan  and  ShenZhen  have  expanded  the investment  of  testing  equipment  for  5G  FR1/FR2.  The  labs  in  KunShan  and ShenZhen have the samples transport to the test fields which are able to operate at any time since the policy  of lockdown  from the local government. Therefore, the company continues to provide holistic verifying service.
- B 、 Subsidiary  Company  in  USA ： The  company  keeps  providing  the  holistic  testing service to local clients and building laboratory for customerized projects.
- C 、 During the covid epidemic, the demand of onling digital platforms, teleconferencing, distance  learning,  distance  medical  care,  unmanned  factories  has  been  increasing, the  company  will  keep  researching  and  developing  5G  NR  terminal  devices acquired by the market. The number of models which have to be tested will also increase, the revene of our company will also increase.

## 2. Passive componentsselling:

The resell of passive components in uur company has gradually become customerization of researching, developing, and manufacturing components to increase the revenue and gross profit of the company.

In  recent  years,  some  of  Taiwan  business,  foreign  business,  China  business  had made mistakes in their testing reports, they had wrong test, missed tests, not give a test, or made a fake report. As a result, their certificate of products and the qualification of having laboratories had been canceled by NCC, FCC, or even MSIT. A bad quality of testing  report  will  cause  our  clients  getting  a  useless  products  certificate  and  having effects on selling products. Sporton as a third-party laboratory has the corporate culture of decency, precision, and honesty in order to be able to stand firm in the testing industry for a long time.

As  expected  last  year,  5G  millimeter  wave  and  Wi-Fi  7  began  to  release  new products at the end of 2022, and began to benefit in early 2023. Since the second half of 2022, due to the effect of inventory destocking of the supply chain, the sales volume of smartphones and notebooks has slowed down. Global consumer electronics manufacturers must continue to launch new products in order to grow profitably. It can

be seen from the market competition of smartphone manufacturers shifting from Sub-6G specification to millimeter wave specification terminal products, and the development of high-end  5G  mobile  notebooks  with  real-time  Internet  connection  in  response  to  the needs of remote work and virtual conferences. Sporton is not affected by inflation. The impact  of  the  boom  and  the  shortage  of  raw  materials  has  been  confirmed  again. Looking forward to the future, with the establishment of 5G+AIOT smart networking, the  generation  of  Wi-Fi  6E/7  and  millimeter  wave  (mmWave)  is  coming.  Related applications  range  from  current  smartphones  and  routers  to  future  vertical  field  small base  stations,  Internet  of  Vehicles,  high-end  mobile  laptops,  medical  networks  and terminal  artificial  intelligence.The  scope  of  applications  such  as  4K/8K  audio-visual streaming  is  expanding.  It  is  expected  that  the  production  of  5G+AIOT  networking products in the future will adopt a small amount of various customized production forms. Therefore, it is expected that the multi-model (Model) type of AIOT products is the most favorable  business  opportunity  for  the  testing  industry.  In  addition  to  the  continuous deployment of 802.11ax WiFi 6E and the latest 802.11be WiFi 7 production capacity and continuous improvement of testing technology, the company also actively participates in forum of the 5G mobile communication technology standard association to  grasp  the  future    AIOT  (Intelligent  Internet  of  Things)  terminal  devices  integrates wireless  communication,  5G  networking  functions  and  digital  technology.  It  brings opportunities of 5G+AIOT testing business in various application scenarioscontinues to make profits  for  the  Company,  and  allows  shareholders,  customers,  and  employees  to share business results.

Finally, we urge all shareholders to continue providing your support and advice, and wish all shareholders healthy body and good luck.

Chairperson: Huang, Wen-Liang

Manager: Huang, Wen-Liang

Accounting Supervisor: Pan, Feng-Wen

(3) Not himself or his spouse, minor children or other people's name with holding more than 1% of company's total issued shares, or as the Top 10 shareholders of the company.

(4) Not a spouse, relative within second degree, or direct blood relative within third degree of the managers listed in (1) or the people listed in (2), (3).

(5) Directors, supervisors, or employees of corporate shareholders who do not directly hold more than 5% of the company's total issued shares or hold the top 5 shares, or designate representatives as company's directors or supervisors on the basis of Article 27-1 or 2 of Company Act. （ Independent directors who concurrently serve in the company and its parent company, subsidiary or subsidiary belonging to the same parent company according to the Act or local regulations are not subject to the restrictions)

- (6) Directors, supervisors or employees of other companies who do not individually hold more than half of the shares related to directors'  seats  or  voting  rights. （ Independent  directors  who  concurrently  serve  in  the  company  and  its  parent  company, subsidiary or subsidiary belonging to the same parent company according to the Act or local regulations are not subject to the restrictions)
- (7) Directors, supervisors, or employees of other companies or institutions who are not the same person or spouse of the company's chairman,  general  manager  or  equivalent. （ Independent  directors  who  concurrently  serve  in  the  company  and  its  parent company, subsidiary or subsidiary belonging to the same parent company according to the Act or local regulations are not subject to the restrictions)
- (8) Directors, supervisors, managers or shareholders holding more than 5% of the company's total shares of specific companies or institutions that do not have financial or business dealings with the company. (In the case a specific company or institution holds  20-50% of total  issued  shares  of  the  company,  independent  directors  who  concurrently  serve  in  the  company  and  its parent company, subsidiary or subsidiary belonging to the same parent company according to the Act or local regulations are not subject to the restrictions)
- (9) Professionals of business, legal, financial, accounting and other relevant services, and owners, partners, directors, supervisors, managers  and  their  spouses  of  sole  proprietorships,  partnerships,  corporations  or  institutions  who  do  not  provide  auditing service for the company or its affiliated companies or do not acquire more than NT$ 500,000 of cumulative remuneration in the last  two  years.  However,  members  of  Remuneration  Committee,  Audit  Committee  for  Public  Tender  Offer,  or  Special Committee for Merger and Acquisition implementing their powers based on the relevant regulations for Securities Exchange Act or Business Mergers And Acquisitions Act are not subject to the restrictions.
- (10) Not in kinship with other directors as spouse, or secondary relatives.
- (11) Not meeting one of the provisions in Article 30 of Company Act.
- (12) Not being elected as the government, legal person or representative under the regulation of article 27 of Company Act.

## 2. Board Diversity and Independence ：

- (1) Board Diversity: Describe the diversity policy, objectives and achievement of the Board of Directors.  The  diversity  policy  includes,  but  is  not  limited  to,  the  criteria  for  election  of directors, the professional qualifications and experience, gender, age, nationality and culture of  the  board  of  directors,  and  the  composition  or  ratio  of  the  board  of  directors,  and describes  the  Company's  specific  objectives  and  their  achievement  with  respect  to  the aforementioned policy.
1. The  Company  advocates  and  respects  the  policy  on  diversity  of  directors  in  order  to strengthen corporate governance and promote the sound development of the composition and structure of the Board of Directors, believing that the diversity approach will help enhance  the  overall  performance  of  the  Company.  The  election  of  board  members  is based  on  the  principle  of  meritocracy  and  is  based  on  the  following  two  general standards:
- (1) Basic requirements and values: Gender, age, nationality, and culture.
- (2) Professional  knowledge  and  skills:  A  professional  background  (e.g.,  accounting, industry, finance, marketing), professional skills, and industry experience.
2. In order to strengthen the functions of the Board of Directors to achieve the ideal goals of corporate governance, the Company considers the following abilities when nominating candidates for directors:
- (1) Ability to make operational judgments.
- (2) Ability to perform accounting and financial analysis.
- (3) Ability to conduct management administration.
- (4) Ability to conduct crisis management.
- (5) Knowledge of the industry.
- (6) An international market perspective.
- (7) Ability to lead.
- (8) Ability to make policy decisions.

3. The Company's current Board of Directors' diversity policy and its implementation are as follows:

The  Company's  current  Board  of  Directors  is  composed  of  7  directors,  including  4 directors  and  3  independent  directors,  in  which  57%  are  employees  and  14%  are females, and the three independent directors have a term of office of 1 to 7 years. The Directors have financial and management qualifications and are professionals in their respective  fields  of  expertise.  4  of  the  directors  are  skilled  in  management  and leadership decision-making, and are professionals in the Company's industry knowledge  and  international  market  perspective,  while  the  other  3  independent directors  are  professionals  in  finance  and  business,  respectively,  and  have  provided extensive  guidance  to  the  Company's  businesses.  The  Company  emphasizes  gender equality  in  the  composition  of  the  Board  of  Directors  and  targets  to  increase  the number of female directors to more than one-third (i.e. 33%) of the Board of Directors. The current Board of Directors is composed of 86% (6) male members and 14% (1) female member, and will strive to increase the number of female directors in the future in order to achieve the target.

- (2) Board Independence: Describe the number and percentage of independent directors, and explain the  independence  of  the  board  of  directors,  with  reasons,  and  state  whether  there  are  no circumstances under paragraphs 3 and 4 of Article 26-3 of the Securities and Exchange Act, including a description of director, supervisor, or any spouse or relative within two degrees of kinship between directors and supervisors.

The Company's 9th Board of Directors has 7 members, including 3 independent directors. The goal is to have no less than 3 independent directors with no less than 1/5 (inclusive) of the total number of directors, and in 2022, there are 3 independent directors with 3/7 of the total number of directors. The number of directors who are concurrently employees of the Company should not  exceed  1/2  of  the  total  number  of  directors,  and  there  are  currently  4  directors  who  are concurrently employees. In addition, more than half of the seats of directors should not consist

of spouses or relatives within the second degree of kinship, etc. Currently, only two directors have spouses or relatives within the second degree of kinship. In summary, except for the goal that the number of directors who are concurrently employees of the Company does not exceed 1/2  of  the  total  number  of  directorships,  all  other  goals  for  director  independence  have  been achieved.

Compare and explain company's and consolidated companies' ratio of total remuneration, paid to directors, supervisors, general managers, deputy general managers, to the net profit after tax  in  the  past  two  years.  And,  explain  the  correlation  between  remuneration  policies, standard, combination, setup procedure and business performance and future risks.
- (1) The analysis of company's and combined companies' ratio of total remuneration, paid to directors, supervisors, general managers, and deputy general managers, to net profit after tax.
- (2) The remuneration policy is based on the scope of the position and the contribution to the company's operational  objectives.  In  addition  to  company's  overall  operational  performance,  the  procedure  of setting  up  remuneration  would  be  based  on  individual  performance,  performance  achievements,  and contribution to the company, and shall be approved by the chairman.
- (3) Policy and standards for remuneration:
1. The  Company's  directors'  remuneration  is  determined  with  reference  to  the  evaluation results of the directors'  performance  evaluation,  and  the  Remuneration  Committee considers the extent of each director's participation in the Company's operations and the value of his or her contribution to the Company's operations, links the reasonableness and fairness of the performance risk to the remuneration received, and makes recommendations to the Board of Directors for approval after taking into account the Company's operational performance and the usual level of payments in the same industry. Except for the directors who are concurrently employees, the Company contributes pensions in accordance with the Labor Standards Act and the Labor Pension Act, the other directors do not have any other

retirement  benefit  system,  and  none  of  the  directors  have  any  severance  pay  or  claim mechanism.

2. The Company's high-level managers are defined at the level of associate manager or above, and are divided into sales and management personnel. In addition to the fixed salary, the senior  managers  are  given  performance  bonuses  based  on  the  achievement  of  each performance target. The procedure for setting the remuneration not only takes into account the  overall  operational  performance  of  the  Company,  but  also  takes  into  account  the individual's performance evaluation, performance achievement rate and contribution to the company's performance, and submits to the chairman for approval.
2. Fixed  salary:  paid  in  accordance  with  the  scope  of  duties  and  responsibilities  of  the position (implementation of business philosophy, organizational leadership and ability to manage, human resources management and talent development, risk management, innovation and integration, compliance with laws and regulations, and sustainability strategies).

Variable  Pay:  based  on  the  achievement  of  various  performance  indicators  (revenue management,  performance  achievement  rate,  personnel  quality  maintenance,  net  profit after tax, customer management and service, and implementation of internal control rules), performance  bonuses  are  calculated  according  to  the  company's  various  bonuses  and employee compensation methods.

Note 5: The assessment content shall include at least the following indicators in accordance with the assessment scope:

- (1) Self-Assessment  of  the  Performance  by  Board  of  Directors:  Shall  include  at  least  the  five  aspects  of  self-assessment, including participation in the operation of the company, the quality of the board of directors' decision making, composition and structure of the Board of Directors, election and continuing education of the directors, and internal control, etc.

(2) Self-Assessment of the Performance by Board Members: Shall include at least the six aspects of self-assessment, including alignment of the goals and missions of the company, awareness of the duties of a director, participation in the operation of the  company,  management  of  internal  relationship  and  communication,  the  director's  professionalism  and  continuing education, and internal control.

(3) Self-Assessment  of  the  Performance  by  Functional  Committee:  Shall  include  at  least  the  five  aspects  of  self-assessment, including  participation in  the  operation  of  the  company,  awareness  of  the  duties  of  the  functional  committee,  quality  of decisions made by the functional committee, makeup of the functional committee and election of its members, and internal control.

3. Report on the Results of the Performance Evaluation of the Board of Directors and the Functional Committee
1. In order to implement corporate governance and enhance the functions of the Board of Directors and Functional Committees, the Company performs performance evaluation of the Board of Directors and Functional Committees in accordance with the Company's "Rules for Performance Evaluation of Board of Directors".
2. The evaluation method is based on a questionnaire, and the questionnaire is categorized into five levels: No. 1: Very poor (strongly disagree); No. 2: Poor (disagree); No. 3: Moderate (average); No. 4: Excellent (agree); No. 5: Excellent (strongly agree); Self-evaluation results are scored out of 100, and the scores are converted into weighted percentages. Scores from 100 to 90 are evaluated  as  excellent,  90  to  80  are  evaluated  as  good,  and  below  80  are  evaluated  as: improvement should be accelerated.

- II. Company's shareholding structure and shareholders' equity

1. Does the company have internal operating procedures handling shareholders' suggestions, doubts, disputes and litigation matters and implement those according to the procedures?

2. Does the company have the list of main shareholders controlling the company and final controller of main shareholders?

3. Does the company establish, execute risk control and firewall mechanism with related companies?

4. Does the company have internal regulation to prevent insiders from using the undisclosed information on the market to buy and sell securities?

V

V

1. The Company has established a spokesperson and a deputy spokesperson, whose contact information is disclosed on the Company's website. The shareholders can raise their opinions through telephones or e-mails, etc. and the Company's dedicated staff will deal with shareholders' suggestions, doubts, disputes and litigations.

2. The Company has maintained a register of major shareholders with controlling power as well as a register of persons exercising ultimate control over those major shareholders.

3. The Company has stipulated relevant control mechanisms and internal control systems in accordance with the law.

4. The Company has stipulated "Procedures for the Prevention of Insider Trading"", which defines the criteria of conduct of the Company's directors, supervisors, managerial officers, and employee, and clearly indicates the laws, regulations, and orders of inside trading that the Company's directors, supervisors, managerial officers, and employee shall abide by.

| ## Assessment Item  Operating Status  Deviations from 'the  Corporate social  responsibilities  practical principles for  TWSE/TPEx Listed  Companies' and  reasons  Yes  No  Abstract Illustration  III    Environmental Issues  1.  Does the company committed to  improve the efficiency of usage of each  resource and to use recycled materials  having low impact on the environment?  2.  Does the company establish a suitable  environmental managing system based  on its industrial characteristics?  3. Ｖ  Ｖ  Ｖ  Ｖ  1.  The company is conducting testing and certification of  electromagnetic compatibility and safety regulations for  electronic and electrical products. All the equipment are  measurement instruments, anechoic chamber, receiving  antennas, personal computers, etc. The company is not  conducting  manufacturing  operation,  which  has  no  major pollution and is not affected by EU (ROHS).  2.  Air-conditioning  apparatus  and  lighting  apparatus  are  the  major  equipment  equipped  in  the  Company's  business  offices.  In  case  the  lamps  are  damaged,  energy-saving  lamps  will  be  the  replacement  of  them.  The  air-conditioning  apparatus  employed  by  the  Company must have energy-saving labels and reminder  signs  are  posted  for  requesting  colleagues  to  set  the  temperature of air-conditioning apparatus as 26 degree  in business offices. Colleagues also take the initiative to  turn  off  the  lights  of  unessential  area  during  non-business hours for reducing carbon emissions.  3.  Investor relations related colleagues communicate with  shareholders  and  potential  investors  through  on-line  phones or video conferences for reducing transportation  and  time  costs,  which  also  decrease  greenhouse  gas  emissions because of transportation.  4.  Apply water saving apparatus for providing full  adjustment of the water output of the faucet  equipment, and use water saving appliances with  water saving labels, including induction type urinals.  5.  The major portion of waste generated by the Company  comes  from  papers  in  offices  and  laboratories.  Currently, the Company is committed to reducing paper  waste  and  recycling  of  resources.  Therefore,  we  sort  and  recycle  the  waste  in  order  to  put  the  waste  reduction and recycling of resources into practice. The  colleagues  sort  the  garbage  in  the  temporary  storage  area  by  themselves  first,  then  the  attendants  of  the  outsourcing property management company handle the  garbage  collection  and  disposal.  In  response  to  the  policy  of  "Prohibition  of  Disposable  and  Melamine  Tableware", three employee cafeteria in Tao Yuan and  Hsin Chu have replaced the disposable lunch boxes and  melamine  tableware  with  environmentally  friendly  tableware in order to not only protect employee health  but  also  implement  waste  reduction,  protect  the  earth,  and create a healthy and e-friendly food culture.  The company is  implementing  operations based on'  the corporate social  responsibilities  practical principles for  TWSE/TPEx Listed  Companies|' without  deviation.  IV  Social Issues  1. 4. In 2021, the Company held the "initiate a team for enjoying a thin life together － weight loss competition" with the limit of five months. The purpose is to enhance the posture together with health, in which unhealthy weight loss methods are prohibited and the weight loss cannot make the BMI less than 18.5. A total of 116 colleagues have participated in this event and the total weight loss is 343.8 kg, which means an average weight loss of 2.9 kg per colleague.

5. In order to promote a smoke-free workplace environment, the Company organized the "No Cigarettes and Win a Lifetime Health" cessation activity with the limit of four months in 2021. A total of 15 colleagues have participated in this event, in which the Company subsidized employees for cessation clinic visits and a total of 4 colleagues has met the cessation criteria.

- 6.The company has stipulated 'Employee education training method' and provides training courses according to duties and professional requirements to promote employees' qualities and business performance.

7. The company has set up 0800 free customer satisfaction line. In addition to handle related matters by designated customer service department, the company will phone customers irregularly to understand customers complaints and satisfaction survey.

8. Please refer to Note (1) for the supplier management policies.

## Note (1)

1. Supplier Code of Conduct

To implement environmental sustainability and fulfill corporate social responsibility, the Company has established  the  "Supplier  Commitment  to  Social  Responsibility"  as  a  principle  and  guideline  for managing  and  selecting  suppliers,  which  specifies  that  the  selection  of  suppliers  should  consider compliance with human rights protection (Declaration of Minerals Conflict-Free, etc.), environmental sustainability  (environmental  permits  and  reports,  pollution  prevention  and  resource  conservation, etc.),  social  norms  (employment  must  be  voluntary  and  child  labor  is  prohibited,  etc.),  and  code  of conduct  (following  the  Code  of  Ethics)  to  ensure  that  suppliers  meet  social  responsibility,  green environmental  protection,  occupational  safety  and  health,  labor  practices,  and  labor  human  rights issues.

The Company requires suppliers with transaction amounting to NT$1 million or above in 2022 to sign the "Supplier Commitment to Social Responsibility" and conduct supplier CSR self-assessment. 100% of the suppliers have signed the commitment and have indicated in the self-assessment that they have met and complied with and fulfilled their CSR.

2. Supplier Sustainability Assessment - Guidelines for Supplier Selection
- (1)  Pre-screening:  Newly  developed  suppliers  are  required  to  audit  in  advance  whether  they  are involved in environmental pollution or endanger social protection, and  are required to sign  a  " Supplier Commitment to Social Responsibility " and be included in the list of suppliers that are regularly audited every year. If the supplier does not pass the inspection, the supplier will not be cooperated with.
- (2)  Proactive  investigation:  For  existing  suppliers,  not  only  do  they  have  to  sign  the  "Supplier Commitment to Social Responsibility", but they are also required to regularly audit whether they are  involved  in  environmental  pollution  or  endangering  social  protection,  etc.  If  there  is  any violation, the supplier will be urged and required to make continuous improvement; if the supplier passes the audit, it will be included in the database of qualified suppliers and will be the preferred target for cooperation by the procurement unit.
- (3)  Commitment  to  Compliance:  Provide  our  "Supplier  Commitment  to  Social  Responsibility", inviting suppliers to respond to issues related to environmental protection and social issues, and to fulfill their responsibility for sustainability.
- (4)  Commitment to use: Suppliers are encouraged to meet green procurement requirements from the procurement  of  materials  to  the  manufacturing  of  goods,  and  are  given  bonus  points  in  the selection of suppliers.
- (5)  Education and training: Through  daily supplier meetings to introduce  and  communicate environmental protection concepts to ensure that suppliers clearly comprehend the importance of environmental protection.
- (6)  Counseling for improvement: Conduct counseling for improvement for unqualified suppliers, and if no improvement is made, terminate the cooperation relationship or reduce the transaction.

## (9) Implementation of internal control system

## ⅰ .Internal Control Statement

## Sporton International Inc.

## Statement of Internal Control System

Date:  February  22,2023

The internal control system in our company in 2022, according to the result of self-assessment is stated as follows:

1. The  company  acknowledges  that  the  establishment,  implementation,  and  maintenance  of  the  internal control  system  are  the  responsibility  of  the  Board  of  Directors  and  managers.  The  company  has established this system. The internal control system is aimed to reasonably assure that the goals such as operations effectiveness and efficiency(including profitability, performance and protection of assets), the reliability, timeliness, transparency and the compliance of relevant regulation and ordinance are achieved.
2. The internal system has its innate restriction. An effective internal control system can only assure the three goals as mentioned above are achieved no matter how perfectly it is designed. Besides, the effectiveness of  internal  control  system  might  be  changed  due  to  the  change  of  environments  and  circumstances. However, the internal control system of the company has self-monitoring function. The company will take action to correct the mistake immediately once it is identified.
3. The company judges whether the design and execution of    internal control system are effective according to  the  judgment  item  of  the  effectiveness  of  internal  control  system  in  '  Standards  for  Publicly  Held Companies to Internal Control Systems'(hereinafter referred to as the Standards). The judgment items of internal  control  system  adopted  by  the  Standards  divide  the  internal  control  system  into  five  elements according to the process of management control: 1. Control environment, 2. Risk assessment, 3. Control Operation, 4. Information and Communication, 5. Supervisory Operation. Each element also includes a certain number of items. The foregoing items please refer to the regulations of the Standards.
4. The company has adopted the judgment items of internal control system as mentioned above to assess the effectiveness of the design and execution of internal control system.
5. Based on the antecedent result of assessment, the company  believes that the internal control system(including the supervisory and control of the subsidiaries)on December 31, 2022 2 can reasonably assure  the  achievement  of  goals,  including  understanding  achievement  level  of  the  effectiveness  and efficiency,  the  report  is  reliable,  timely  and  transparent  and  comply  with  the  relevant  regulation  and ordinance, and whether the design and execution of internal control system are effective.
6. This statement  will  be  the  primary  content  of  the  company's  annual  report  and  prospectus  and  will  be disclose to the public. If the contents that be disclose are illegal, such as falsehood and concealment, it will involve in legal liability, such as Article 20, 32, 171, and 174 of Securities and Exchange Law.
7. This  statement  has  been  approved  by  the  Board  of  Directors  onFebruary  22,  2023.  There  are  zero  in directors in presence reject the statement and the others all agree with the content of the statement.

Sporton International Inc. Chairman: Huang, Wen-Liang

General  Manager:  Huang,  Wen-Liang

- Remark  1:If  the  design  and  execution  of  the  internal  control  system  of  public company have significant deficiency in the  year, it should be listed in the  statement  of  the  internal  control  system.  Section  No.4  and  its following section are the additional items that enumerate and illustrate the actions taken before the date of assets liabilities and situation for the significant deficiency found in self-testing.

Remark 2: The date of the statement is the end date of fiscal year.

*If there are dividends from increasing earnings or capital reserve, the company should disclose the information of market price and cash dividend adjusted according to the issued shares.

Note 1: Shows the highest and lowest market price of ordinary shares in each year, and the average annual market price is calculated based on the meeting turnover and volume.

Note 2: Based on the shares issued at the end of the year and list according to the distribution decided by next annual shareholders meeting.

Note 3: If there is an adjustment due to the situation, such as bonus shares, the earnings before and after the adjustment should be listed.

Note 4: If it has stipulated that unissued dividend can be accumulated until the year that earnings issued in the terms of issuing equity securities, the unpaid dividend as of the year should be disclosed separately.

Note 5: Price / Earnings ratio = average closing price per shares for the year / earnings per share

Note 6: Price / Dividend ratio = average closing price per shares for the year / cash dividend per share

Note7: Cash dividend yield rate = cash dividend per share / average closing price per shares for the year

Note8: The information audited by the accountant as of the Date of Publication of the Annual Report and in last quarter should be listed for the Net worth per share and earnings per share: the annual information as of the Date of Publication of the Annual Report should be listed in another fields.

Note9: Waiting for the resolution of Annual General Meeting in 2023.

- (6) The Company's Dividend Policy and Implementation Status
- (1) Dividend Policy

If there are earnings in the General Budget Final Account of the company, the company should  pay  tax  and  make  up  the  accumulating  loss  first.  Second,  lodging  10%  of  legal reserve.    Moreover,  after  listing  and  reversing  the  special  reserve,  the  balance  adds beginning  retained  earnings  can  be  available  for  appropriated  earnings.  The  Board  of Directors  will  draw  up  the  proposal  of  profit  distribution  according  to  No.2  of  the dividend policy, and propose the shareholder's meeting to decide the dividend and bonus for the shareholders. The company's dividend policy is to assess the future capital budget of  the  company, plan the future fund needed, financial structure, and earnings, etc. The company is in a stable growth stage. In order to continue expending the scale to maintain a  sustainable operation  and stable  growth, the dividend distribution based on the future operating scale and the demand of cash flow. The dividend distribution has to be more than 40% of the earnings that available for distributing in the  year, and it can be stock dividend or cash dividend. In addition, the ratio of cash dividend has to be no less than 10% of the total amount of the dividend.

- (2) To maintain the position leading the industry, the company will keep purchasing advanced testing  techniques  for  the  demand  in  the  future.  Thus,  the  Annual  General  Meeting propose to distribute 50 shares  gratuitously allotted  for  every  thousand  shares  and  11.0 dollars per share for cash dividend. The total amount of profit distribution is constant, but the amount of distribution per share will be changed following the issued shares on the standard  date  dividend  distribution.  Based  on  the  amount  of  the  resolution  of  profit distribution proposal, the Chairman was authorized to adjust the ratio of the payments of the  shareholders  according  to  the  actual  issued  shares  on  the  standard  date  of  dividend distribution.

(8) Bonuses of Employees, Directors and Supervisors

1. The percentage or scope of the bonuses of Employees, Directors and Supervisors stipulated in the Articles of Association. If there is a profit for the year, the company should contribute 1%  to  13.5%  to  be  bonus  for  employees.  The  Board  of  Directors  Meetings decides  to payout with stock or cash. Targets of payment includes the employees comply with certain term.  The  company  can  exceed  the  profit  amount  and  the  Broad  of  Directors  Meetings decides to contribute no more 2% as bonuses of Directors and Supervisors, which can only be paid out with cash. However, if there is still a cumulative loss, an amount to make up for the loss should be preserved in advanced. Next, the bonuses of Employees, Directors and Supervisors are contributed in accordance with the former ratio.
2. The basis of the estimation of the amount of bonus of employees, directors and supervisors in  the  current  period,  and  the  accounting  treatment  if  there  is  a  difference  between  the actual employee bonuses paid in shares and the estimated amount.

The basis of the estimation of the amount of bonus of employees, directors and supervisors in the current period is based on the percentage stipulated in the Article as of the profit of the period (pre-tax profit deduct the profit before assign for bonus of employees, Directors and Supervisors).The basis accounting of actual employee bonuses paid in shares is decided by the amount of assignation division by fair value of stock. Fair value of stock refers to the closing  price  before  the  date  of  Board  of  Directors  Meetings'  resolution.  If  there  is  a difference between actual amounts assigned and estimated amount, it will be handled by the accountant and decided to adjust annually during the Board of Directors Meetings.

3. Bonus distributions approved by the Board of Directors Meetings:
- A.  Distribution  of  bonus  of  employees  in  cash  or  stock  and  amount  of  Directors  and Supervisors' bonus. If there is a difference between the fee and the annual estimated amount, the company should disclose the amount of difference, reasons, and handling status.

The  fee  of  bonuses  of  Employees,  Directors  and Supervisors  has  been  conducted  since  2022.  The  company  has  carried  the  fee  of bonuses of Employees, Directors and Supervisors. ## (2) Industry overview

1. Industry status and development

The Internet of Things (IOT) has opened up a new global industry, and AIoT (AI+IoT), as its name implies, is the introduction of AI artificial intelligence systems into IoT technology. 5G+AIOT is the most important key technology to connect the global  industry  into  the  era  of  intelligent  networking  with  mobile  communication networks. The recent popularity of Chat GPT and Edge AI represents that all global industries  have  officially  entered  the  era  of  intelligent  networking.  The  telecom industry's  5G  networking  infrastructure  continues  to  scale  up  and  expand  rapidly. The  effect  of  penetration  of  various  types  of  connected  terminals  to  all  levels  of industry  also continues to ferment. If the previous stage of industrial upgrading is "digital transformation", then this stage of industrial upgrading is "AIoT". It can be found that all countries, no matter what types of industries or government units, are closely  watching  the  development  process  of  5G+AIOT  technology  evolution, fearing that they will lose their competitiveness in the industry in the future if they do  not  keep  up  in  this  global  industrial  transformation  and  upgrade.  Under  the premise  of  Moore's  Law,  the  development  of  digital  technology  is  now  at  a

high-speed stage of exponential growth and continuous evolution. The downstream of  the  core  industry  application  of  5G+AIOT,  the  intelligent  networking  testing laboratory will be a very important link to promote the upgrade of the industry. With long  years  of  experience  in  mobile  communication  testing  and  wireless  network testing, the Company has become a cross-generation laboratory providing complete solutions for IoT endpoint testing.

The Company's main business items are electromagnetic compatibility certification of electrical and electronic products, safety inspection services, wireless networking certification, mobile phone and 5G interconnect end product certification and passive component sales. It is a next-generation testing laboratory that provides a  complete  solution  for  5G+AIOT  examination  and  certification  when  producing various smart interconnect products in response to the global industrial transformation and upgrade into the era of smart IOT.

The complete examination solution for the new generation of smart networking products  is  as  follows:  EMC  (Electromagnetic  Compatibility)  examination,  which mainly  includes  the  reduction  of  electromagnetic  interference  (EMI)  and  the improvement  of  electromagnetic  tolerance  (EMS)  of  electrical  and  electronic products.

Safety Inspections, or product safety certification, for any material properties, structures,  and  usage  of  products  that  are  likely  to  cause  electric  shock,  energy hazards, fire, mechanical and heat hazards, radiation hazards, and chemical hazards, should be included in the scope of safety inspections.

Radio &amp; Mobile Communication, the wireless communication technology field includes low-power RF SRD (Short Range Device) which refers to all low-power, widely interconnected Internet of Things (IOT) technologies, and WLAN (Wireless LAN) which is a wireless technology under the IEEE 802.11 wireless LAN standard. The latest standard is 802.11ax Wi-Fi 6E 2.4GHz/5GHz/6GHz tri-band application. The next generation of regional broadband network standard Wi-Fi 7, also known as IEEE 802.11be EHT (Extremely  High Throughput), will  come  along  with  it.  The mobile  broadband  network  WWAN  (Wireless  Wide  Area  Network)  refers  to  the wireless wide area network, which is currently progressing to the fifth generation of mobile communication technology under the guidance of 3GPP standards organization,  including  FR1  (Sub  6GHz)  and  FR2  millimeter  wave  (mmWave) applications.

The fifth-generation mobile communication technology 5G New Radio (5GNR) adopts  MIMO  (Multiple-Input  Multiple-Output)  multiple  input,  multiple  output antenna technology for ultra-high-speed, low-latency, and wide-connection Internet of Everything environment, and applies to two frequency bands at the same time: the frequency range between 450MHz and 6GHz for Sub 6G, and the frequency range between  24GHz  and  52GHz  for  extremely high frequency millimeter  wave (mmWave). In addition to the complexity and difficulty of the latest 5G protocol test items,  5G  NR  test  laboratory  needs  to  have  both  compulsory  regulatory  tests required  by  government  agencies,  OTA  performance  tests  required  by  CTIA organizations and European and American telecom operators, and FTA conformance tests  required  by  PTCRB/GCF  organizations.  Only  testing  laboratory  that  can provide  testing  solutions  for  5G  mobile  communication  products  in  the  aforesaid three  categories  can  be  called  a  "5G  NR  Testing  Laboratory"  with  complete measurements, whether for 5G mobile phones, 5G CPE/FWA, 5G mobile laptops,

5G modules, and other  products,  and  Sporton  International  Inc.  is  one  of  the  few testing laboratories in the industry with complete solutions for all  5G  NR measurements.

In addition to the mobile phones and 5G networking end products, in the part of regional broadband networking product testing, the Wi-Fi 6E tri-band era from last year  has  been  upgraded  to  the  higher-end  802.11  be  Wi-Fi  7  generation.  Besides extending  the  application  to  the  6GHz  band  from  the  original  2.4GHz  and  5GHz bands,  Wi-Fi  7  further  upgrades  the  modulation  technology  and  bandwidth  and MIMO  technology  from  the  original  Wi-Fi  6/6E  stage  with  a  maximum  of  8 antennas  to  16  antennas,  which  will  be  the  world's  fastest  commercial  wireless transmission rate solution. These specifications will bring multi-mode, multi-antenna, multi-channel  complex  testing  project  mode  and  increase  the  number  of  testing hours, which will greatly enhance network communication testing business revenue and profit growth. The Company has once again taken the lead in the latest Wi-Fi 7 testing market. In early December 2022, we assisted Broadcom to obtain the world's first  Wi-Fi  7  FCC  certificate,  and  in  early  February  2023,  we  further  collaborated with ASUS to obtain the world's first FCC certificate for Wi-Fi 7 indoor low power router (AP). With the growth trend of 5G NR dual engine, it will continue to provide strong growth momentum for the Company's operation.
